- evidence: crates/agenttrace-core/src/parser.rs:32-40 bails with "decompress it to JSONL first" at HEAD 90a4ef5; Codex latest release rust-v0.159.2 published 2026-09-29 (GitHub API, fetched 2026-09-30, research pass 7); local codex 0.147.0 still writes JSONL, so the defect is forward compatibility for mainstream-current CLI users
- notes: cross-campaign corroboration (2026-10-03, run 0d487394, research e053118a at HEAD 5ef66c0): upstream shipped the compression this item anticipated — openai/codex#25089 'Compress cold local rollouts' merged 2026-06-01 (writers keep plain .jsonl; cold sessions become .jsonl.zst; disabled-by-default worker compresses archives, so the compressed share grows over time by default). Fresh PoC with the release binary at 5ef66c0 adds a second failure mode to this item's scope: a literal .jsonl.zst file is INVISIBLE to discovery ('Error: No session files found', rc 1, no hint — /tmp/at-assess-0c01b29f/z1-out.txt), while a zstd-framed file named .jsonl hits the documented manual-decompress bail (z2-out.txt). Acceptance should therefore cover BOTH discovery listing of *.jsonl.zst AND in-process decode; ruzstd stays the right crate (preserves #285's static-CRT story).

### Price multi-model sessions per model

- id: rm-020
- track: correctness
- priority: high
- status: candidate
- signals: correctness.single_model_pricing:crates/agenttrace-core/src/lib.rs::L510, correctness.discarded_model_data:crates/agenttrace-core/src/lib.rs::L147, correctness.misleading_provenance:crates/agenttrace-core/src/lib.rs::L770
- acceptance: Analysis accumulates a per-model token ledger keyed on per-event model_used; session cost is the sum over models of lookup_price(model) x that model's tokens; report --overview, JSON output, and the TUI session detail view render a per-model breakdown (model, tokens in/out, rate source, cost); provenance.cost becomes a per-model enumeration or an explicit mixed-model marker; a golden test pins a two-model fixture's expected per-model costs; the sqlite_sessions.rs multi-model acknowledgment test is updated to assert the new exact behavior
- evidence: Anchors re-verified at HEAD 90a4ef5: lib.rs:509-521 retains one model string per session (per-event model_used captured at :147/:344 is discarded at pricing time), lib.rs:596-601 does one lookup_price, lib.rs:763-772 applies that single price to all tokens and stamps provenance.cost=calculated_from_tokens at :770; sqlite_sessions.rs:857 test admits the analogous imprecision on the SQLite path. Research PE-1 (pass 8, 92% confidence): no competitor prices multi-model sessions per-model (ccusage 18,811 stars, tokenmaxxing both apply one rate), making this both the residual correctness defect (assess AR2-1) and a differentiating capability
- next-cycle anchor (compound c1): top unselected alternate — deferred value-based on merge contention (render surface overlaps 02993de2's staged report_overview_* edits), not on value; becomes the top cycle-2 anchor once that merge lands, with rm-021 riding its per-model ledger
- cycle-1 selection note (2026-09-30, run 0a279c44): passed over again, now solely on effort (M-L across ledger+report/JSON/TUI+goldens) and fresh render-surface churn — the contention is gone (both merges landed); cycle-2 anchor status reaffirmed

### Sanitize statusline report output (terminal control injection from journal payloads)
- id: `rm-034` | track: reliability | priority: 89.0 | status: done
- signals: security.terminal_injection:crates/agenttrace-core/src/statusline.rs::L615+L628+L636-644 (report path prints journal-derived session_id and miss_cause strings raw; the render path sanitizes the same class at L263-268 sanitize_line_segment; ingestion stores hostile payloads verbatim at L275 append_statusline_capture; live repro 2026-09-30: crafted journal payload emitted raw ANSI SGR + OSC-52 through `agenttrace --statusline-report`)
- acceptance: every string printed by --statusline-report passes the same control-character sanitization as the render path (shared helper), locked by a regression test asserting ESC/OSC sequences appear sanitized in report output; TUI panel stays numeric-only
- evidence: cargo test green including the new injection regression test; live repro re-run shows no raw ESC bytes in report output (cat -v); conductor validation digest validation:v1:<sha> recorded in the shipping PR — compound-c1 (2026-09-30, run 88feec46): implemented uncommitted at 7bb4dcb — all three report print sites route through the shared sanitizer (statusline.rs:619/:632/:645 over sanitize_line_segment:265); regression test statusline_report_sanitizes_journal_derived_strings (statusline.rs:909); targeted+full gates green 238/0, digest validation:v1:9c9a1d4d5e3067b03657b0b4984c45be5b0358487a19497c64241c6224082088; live cat -v re-run shows U+FFFD and zero raw ESC; learning recorded at docs/solutions/security-issues/terminal-injection-through-journal-derived-statusline-strings.md; done-flip reserved for the commit gate — flipped 2026-09-30 (commit gate, run 88feec46 cycle 1)
- evidence refresh (2026-10-06, run 90f54faf research 4db229a3, fetched changelog): external-ecosystem precedent — claude-code 2.1.290 "Fixed the background daemon's log passing terminal control characters to the screen under `claude daemon logs`; they now show as \uXXXX escapes": the market leader shipped the same control-byte fix class for daemon-logged output. Corroborates this row's done family and the live unclaimed arms (460d0633's rm-594 counter-key cap + rm-595 counts/disclosure renderer arms; 1ecd791b's landed rm-594 upstream to_text sanitize, committed ae72772).

- evidence refresh (2026-10-03, run 75ae7fb6 assess 4d763916 at 5ef66c0, anchor re-verified at fd5532f): live PoC pins the failure in both directions — a session stamped 2026-10-01T23:00:00Z (= 2026-10-02 08:00 JST, unambiguously today for a UTC+9 user) is silently EXCLUDED from `--range today` while present under `--range all` (corpus /tmp/at-assess-4d763916/poc/sessdir-tz), and west-of-UTC users symmetrically get yesterday-evening sessions counted into today; the only disclosure is the "Range: today" label (insights.rs since(): Today => now.date_naive().and_utc() = UTC midnight, single call site main.rs range.since(Utc::now()))

- evidence refresh (2026-10-05, run 6727d49a assess bd047148 @b01edcd, live): NaN operand re-verified — `--sessions --health <nan` parses, every comparison is false, the filter silently passes ALL sessions rc0; extend acceptance: non-finite operands rejected as loud usage errors
- compound c2 2026-10-05 (run 6727d49a): NOT implemented this cycle. The implement artifact (6906428b) claimed a split_filter/valid_number_filter rewrite of matches_number with 2 unit tests, but tree forensics disproved every marker (main.rs mtime = worktree creation, 0 split_filter/valid_number_filter hits, claimed live PoCs from a binary older than the session); the defect stands exactly as the refresh above records. ### Byte-true session-cache bound and a first dirs bound
- id: `rm-298` | track: reliability | priority: 88.0 | status: implemented
- signals: reliability.bound_violated:crates/agenttrace-core/src/session_cache.rs::L44-49 (MAX_SESSION_CACHE_BYTES 64MiB documented as a "Hard bound on the serialized sessions.json size") vs its own write path — fresh isolated-cache PoC on the real corpus (assess c22757c9): AGENTTRACE_SESSION_CACHE_DIR set, `agenttrace --overview -f json` wrote sessions.json at 72,257,626 B = 108.9% of the 67,108,864 B cap; enforce_byte_bound (:836-863) sizes cache_paths_sized_once values only (:774-836) while save_session_cache (:874-921) also writes per-path keys (~5,293 × ~80 B), JSON punctuation, top-level fields, and the whole dirs map — 3,586,620 B across 2,783 dirs (5.3%, entirely uncounted); dirs has NO count or byte bound of its own (only entries is bounded by MAX_SESSION_CACHE_ENTRIES); in-repo docs flag the class as latent (docs/decisions/2026-09-14-cycle-7-batch-selection.md:47, docs/stewardship/2026-09-03-cycle6-independent-review.md:116) — actual now on a real user corpus; the A11-2 dedup fix landed in the same delta closed only the double-count axis; fills the F3 cycle-2 lead named in the header with no committed wall item
- acceptance: the bound is enforced over exactly the bytes the writer emits (values + path keys + structural JSON + dirs) or the writer truncates to the budgeted bytes, so a fresh full-corpus cache write measures ≤ cap on disk, byte-exact; dirs gains its own count and byte bound; eviction order is deterministic and pinned by a test that builds an over-capacity fixture and asserts the WRITTEN FILE's size ≤ cap (not the estimator's model of it); the real-corpus run on this host lands ≤ 64 MiB
- evidence: unit/property tests green including the over-capacity fixture; a recorded fresh-corpus run showing sessions.json byte size ≤ 67,108,864; the decision doc's "latent" framing corrected in the same change; conductor validation digest validation:v1:<sha> recorded in the shipping PR; CYCLE-1 IMPLEMENTED pre-review (run 364aa3be, implement attempt cfee1fa4, worktree run-364aa3be00f6 @ fd5532f): byte-true projection serialized_doc_size (entry values + per-path keys + JSON punctuation + top-level fields + dirs map, decoded-copy-overwrites-raw modeled) with enforce_byte_bound enforcing the cap over it (fast path = one projection, no member bookkeeping); cache_paths_sized_once now returns WRITTEN-form sizes; new enforce_dirs_bound + MAX_SESSION_CACHE_DIRS=20,000 + MAX_SESSION_CACHE_DIR_BYTES=cap/8; red→green fixture byte_bound_covers_the_written_document_not_a_model (pre-fix RED: written 16,910 B vs 13,112 B ceiling = 129%, the same defect class as the live 108.9%); projection pinned byte-exact by serialized_doc_size_predicts_the_written_file_exactly (escaped keys, both-maps path, stray-field raw copy, dirs); dirs bounds pinned by dirs_map_gains_count_and_byte_bounds_of_its_own; live gate on the real corpus (fresh isolated cache, release binary): sessions.json = 67,107,729 B = 99.998% of 67,108,864 cap (was 72,262,482 B = 108.9% pre-fix), 4,375 of 5,293 entries kept after oldest-first eviction, dirs 2,856 listings / 4,091,271 B ≤ 8 MiB budget; decision-doc "latent" + independent-review F2 stale lines corrected same-change; validation: cargo test -p agenttrace-core 128+7 green, -p agenttrace 72+20+11+9+2 green, fmt+clippy -D warnings rc0, check-deterministic-output + check-output-contract + check-plugin-version gates rc0 with the new binary; changes uncommitted in the worktree awaiting the commit phase
- integrated 2026-10-04 (conflict case 9eec1194, run 364aa3be/3905cea into b1a289b): landed via merge with zero hunk overlap — HEAD made no session_cache.rs change since fd5532f, so the byte-true projection + enforce_dirs_bound arrived verbatim; independent review b890ca0b APPROVED with the cap gate re-reproduced fresh on the grown corpus (sessions.json 67,096,783 B = 99.982% of the 67,108,864 B cap, 4,109 entries kept, dirs 4,183,434 B ≤ 8 MiB sub-budget, schema 21 intact); done-flip remains reserved for the commit gate per convention
- residual (2026-10-05, run 66a7d797 assess d282b81f): the 64 MiB byte-true bound is landed, but rotation re-parses evicted entries on every warm pass at ~13k-file corpora — rotation-thrash is unquantified; revisit the bound (or add a re-parse counter) when corpus sizes are next measured

- cycle-3 corroboration (2026-10-04, run 16bbd3ae assess 428195d6 A5): oldest-mtime eviction re-parses a stable prefix every run at over-bound sizes — this item's own live gate recorded 4,375 of 5,293 entries kept (~918 re-parsed per run); deterministic eviction order is NOT non-thrashing; fold the thrash axis into rm-298/rm-299 when either next opens

- id: `rm-299` | track: performance | priority: 78.0 | status: candidate

- acceptance: a persisted snapshot keyed by a stat-only fingerprint (dev/ino/mtime/size per corpus file) makes repeat invocation on an unchanged corpus complete in single-digit seconds for the default report surface; any corpus mutation (or fingerprint mismatch) forces correct re-derivation, pinned by a mutation-correctness gate (touch one file → affected rows re-derived; restore → cache hits again); the snapshot is offline-only (no network) and covered by cache_artifact_paths() and PRIVACY.md's artifact table from day one (no rm-202-class orphan)
- evidence: before/after timing on the real corpus recorded; mutation-correctness test green; registry + privacy tests extended and green; conductor validation digest validation:v1:<sha> recorded in the shipping PR
- integrated 2026-10-04 (same case 9eec1194): definition landed unchanged; the fold counterpart rm-286 is still UNLANDED anywhere (worktree claim only), so the fold-at-integration instruction stays live — whichever lands first absorbs the other with the union of evidence

- signals: correctness.greedy_decode:crates/agenttrace-core/src/insights.rs::L206-236 (decode_agent_project_dir commits to the FIRST existing directory for each dash-split segment and never backtracks, so any sibling directory that is a dash-prefix of the true name hijacks the decode — live PoC: transcript filed under .../-code-my-app-tools attributed to project my-app when my-app and my-app/tools both exist), compatibility.official_format (code.claude.com/docs/en/sessions, fetched 2026-10-03: encoded names over 200 chars are truncated to 200 + suffixed with a hash of the full path — the decoder has no hash handling and lands on 'unknown'; CLAUDE_CODE_PROJECT_DIR_NAME (v2.1.234+) allows opaque 1-64 [a-zA-Z0-9_-] custom names with CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR that decode to no path at all; /cd relocation v2.1.169+ moves sessions across dirs)
- acceptance: decode explores candidate segment splits with backtracking (DFS) instead of first-existing-dir commitment; a sibling-prefix fixture (my-app + my-app/tools + encoded my-app-tools session) attributes to my-app-tools; the >200-char truncate+hash form decodes by reconstructing the truncation and treating the hash suffix as opaque-but-stable; CLAUDE_CODE_PROJECT_DIR_NAME-style opaque names yield a stable project id instead of 'unknown'; unit tests pin all three cases plus the existing clean cases
- evidence: assess 0c01b29f F2 + research e053118a C2 at HEAD 5ef66c0 (PoC /tmp/at-assess-0c01b29f/misattr/ + exa capture of the official doc); full suite green; conductor validation digest validation:v1:<sha> recorded in the shipping PR
- integrated 2026-10-04 (same case c7f1fc78): the greedy first-existing-dir half of this item landed independently at HEAD as rm-381 (run cf755698) — decode_agent_project_dir (re-anchored insights.rs:250) is now a budget-bounded deterministic DFS with cwd-first order and a shadowed-alternative disclosure surfaced through --doctor's project_decode block, and the sibling-prefix decoy PoC this item cites is pinned by landed tests. The item stays candidate for its DISTINCT residual scope, which rm-381 does not cover: the official >200-char truncate+hash encoded form and CLAUDE_CODE_PROJECT_DIR_NAME opaque names (both still decode to 'unknown'/'unresolved' today).

### Bound the delivery-evidence git subprocess

- id: `rm-242` | track: reliability | priority: 67.0 | status: implemented
- signals: reliability.unbounded_subprocess:crates/agenttrace-core/src/governance.rs::L780 (delivery_evidence_with_git spawns git log --all per distinct session-derived project root with no timeout — a PATH stand-in sleeping 8s blocked --delivery-evidence the full 8s and still exited 0; the exec directory is selected by session cwd, so a stalled mount or hung git wedges the report; 50k-commit repo costs 0.4s/root, so cost scales with distinct roots); same class as the bounded-curl treatment pricing fetch already carries
- acceptance: the git invocation runs under a bounded timeout consistent with the pricing-fetch cap class; on timeout the evidence record degrades soft (level recorded with an explicit timeout annotation, report still completes, exit code unchanged for non-CI lanes); a stand-in sleeping-git test pins the bound; the per-root subprocess budget is documented in the governance guide alongside the existing read-only disclosure
- evidence: assess 0c01b29f F4 at HEAD 5ef66c0 (sleep-git PoC out-slowgit.txt, 50k-commit repo timing); full suite green; conductor validation digest validation:v1:<sha> recorded in the shipping PR
- cycle-2 (2026-10-03, run 0d487394649847ac88e657bb30b4c154, pre-review): EXECUTED — implement b320bd3f at base 5ef66c0 (uncommitted, 7 M + 1 new file): governance.rs now derives roots first (git_commits_by_root) and spawns `git log --all --format=%ct` per root under the new GIT_PROBE_TIMEOUT = Duration::from_secs(10) via wait_child_bounded(child, bound) — try_wait polling + kill on deadline, stdout drained on a helper thread so a full pipe cannot turn a fast child into a spurious timeout; on overrun the root degrades soft through the existing Option path to the tool-authority heuristic (no unclean-ExitStatus panic) and the methodology string discloses 'bounded at 10 seconds per repository root; a probe that overruns the bound is treated as unavailable and its root degrades to the tool-authority heuristic' (governance.rs:751-755 area). Sleeping-git stand-in (PATH git = sleep 30): report completes real 0m10.025s, rc0, degraded evidence. Validated by targeted 2f587f05 + full 04ec205d: fmt rc0, clippy rc0, cargo test core 119+8+7+72+2 / cli 22+10+4+9 / tui 46 = 299 passed 0 failed (full suite mirrored verbatim from .github/workflows/ci.yml job `full`), gates output-contract/deterministic/report-semantics/release-surfaces/install-runtime rc0. Umbrella remainder still open: upstream.rs:185/:201 lanes + rm-050 pricing body read.

### Positional session path must not hijack --overview/--search and silently skip the quality gates
- id: `rm-246` | track: reliability | priority: 91.0 | status: done
- signals: correctness.gate_bypass:crates/agenttrace-cli/src/main.rs::L378-395 (single-session branch fires on args.path.is_some() without excluding --overview/--search and returns before the search branch :428 and the overview/gate branch :440; validate_primary_action :1167-1196 accepts path+action as a legal combination), docs contract:README::L148-158 documents flags-before-positional as a supported invocation
- acceptance: when an explicit action flag (--overview/--search/--sessions/--diagnostics) is present, a positional path is routed to that action — or the combination exits with an explicit conflict error naming both — never silently rendered as a single-session report; `--overview --fail-under-health 100 <file.jsonl>` on a health-40 fixture exits 2 exactly as the -d form does; `--search <query> <file.jsonl>` performs the search; a regression test pins the positional-path gate rc against the -d control

- compound c3 2026-10-03: implemented (cycle-3 batch 'The CLI does what it says' = rm-246 + rm-247 + rm-250, implement fcc9c7aa @5ef66c0, 4-file delta preserved uncommitted): extracted `single_session_report_requested(&Args)` adding `args.search.is_none() && !args.overview` to the exclusion set — the default single-session report renders only when NO action is requested, so a positional path routes into `load_sessions_report` (path-as-file) and overview/search act on it (gate evaluated); regression test `single_session_report_yields_to_explicit_actions`; release-binary PoC flip `--overview --fail-under-health 100 <bad.jsonl>` rc0→rc2 'Gate failed: average health 40.0 is below 100' with the `-d` control byte-identical, `--search tests <file>` now the tool-match table. ### Ignore relative XDG_CACHE_HOME per the basedir spec
- id: `rm-248` | track: compatibility | priority: 74.0 | status: candidate
- signals: compat.xdg_relative:crates/agenttrace-core/src/session_cache.rs::L1141-1147 + statusline user_cache_dir (XDG_CACHE_HOME consumed as-is after only an emptiness check; the XDG Base Directory Specification requires relative values be ignored/treated as unset)
- acceptance: every artifact-root constructor that reads XDG_CACHE_HOME ignores relative values (falling back to HOME/.cache), or accepts them only with a doctor-visible warning; a test pins that `XDG_CACHE_HOME=rel-cache agenttrace …` from an empty cwd creates nothing under ./rel-cache; PRIVACY.md's artifact table stays truthful about where artifacts live
- evidence: live PoC 2026-10-03 (assess 0bc54992 N3 @5ef66c0): XDG_CACHE_HOME=rel-cache from an empty dir → ./rel-cache/agenttrace/sessions.json created in CWD, rc0, silent; consequences measured/reasoned: per-cwd cache duplication (sessions.json rewrite scales with corpus — 72 MB on the real host corpus), --clear-cache issued from another cwd cannot reach the artifact, read-only cwd degrades silently to cold scans; full suite green + conductor validation digest validation:v1:<sha> recorded in the shipping PR

### Disclose discovery-walk failures and fix the -d <file> error message
- id: `rm-249` | track: correctness | priority: 70.0 | status: candidate
- signals: correctness.silent_skip:crates/agenttrace-core/src/discovery.rs::L182+L514+L577 (fs::read_dir failures vanish — discovery has no warn channel, so an unreadable directory silently narrows coverage), correctness.misleading_error:crates/agenttrace-cli/src/main.rs::L861-863 ('No session files found in <path>' reused for a permission-denied root and for -d pointing at a single session file)
- acceptance: unreadable directories are reported (count + path, non-fatal, on stderr and in the load report's skip disclosure) instead of silently narrowing coverage; `-d <unreadable-root>` errors distinguish permission denial from absence with a check-permissions hint; `-d <file.jsonl>` either scans that file or errors that -d takes a directory (pointing at the positional form); fixtures pin the chmod-000-subdir and -d-file cases

- notes: review 3fb3e39d F5 folded here (2026-10-03, pre-existing): `-d <dir> <file>` silently ignores `-d` — the positional path wins (P13: `--overview -d <emptydir> <file>` → rc0, 1 session). Same "no silent flag loss" family as this item's -d error-message work; fix when rm-249 lands.

### Atomic -o report writes
- id: `rm-250` | track: reliability | priority: 60.0 | status: done
- signals: reliability.torn_write:crates/agenttrace-cli/src/main.rs::L905-914 (fs::write directly to the destination, unlike the session cache's unique_temp_path+rename session_cache.rs::L346-368 and history.rs::L47-54 — a crash or Ctrl-C mid-write leaves a truncated report at the target)
- acceptance: -o writes go through temp+rename (reusing the existing unique-temp helper) so a crash leaves the previous file intact and no partial file ever appears at the destination; the 'Saved:' line still prints after the rename; a test pins the atomic path by construction (temp sibling cleaned on every exit path)
- evidence: assess 0bc54992 N7 (code-read @5ef66c0); the parity helpers already exist in-tree — this is adoption, not new machinery; full suite green + conductor validation digest validation:v1:<sha> recorded in the shipping PR
- compound c3 2026-10-03: implemented (same cycle-3 batch, implement fcc9c7aa @5ef66c0): `write_output` stages content in `unique_temp_sibling(path)` (name.tmp.PID.SEQ, mirrors core's session_cache::unique_temp_path — helper is pub(crate) and core is sibling-owned, so the pattern is duplicated in cli) then renames; temp removed on write OR rename failure; the 'Saved:' line prints only after successful rename; test `write_output_stages_atomically_and_leaves_no_temp_residue` (content correct, destination-is-directory rename failure still errors AND cleans, zero `.tmp.` residue). Gates (recorded evidence, not re-run at compound): targeted 46/0 rc0 (e8211212); full 284/0 rc0 + fmt/clippy/locked legs rc0 (c9abad84); digest validation:v1:ed7fc27031119b7016a55b56a385c882d068a24f803eff24a9e467a84d4a0e90. status → done reserved for the commit gate — flipped 2026-10-04 (commit gate, run a1cafb4c1a22 cycle 3).
- review 3fb3e39d F4 (informational, no change): SIGKILL mid-write leaves the .tmp.PID.SEQ sibling behind (cleanup runs only on error paths) and there is no fsync before rename — process-crash safe, not power-loss durable; destination never torn (acceptance core holds); identical to the adopted session_cache/history in-tree pattern.

### Torn-read hardening for the scanner
- id: `rm-254` | track: reliability | priority: 45.0 | status: candidate
- signals: reliability.torn_read:discovery parses whole files mid-scan while the cache fingerprints by (size, mtime) alone (session_cache.rs is_fresh) — a writer rewriting a transcript underneath the scan yields a torn parse or a poisoned fingerprint; upstream evidence: Claude Code CHANGELOG 2.1.288 fixed 'resume occasionally loading a transcript cut short when the same session rewrote the file during the load' — upstream treats the race as real
- acceptance: on parse failure the scanner re-reads once when mtime changed during the read (or reads to end then verifies stable mtime before fingerprinting); a fixture that rewrites the file between stat and parse parses cleanly on the retry; the cache never fingerprints a file whose mtime moved during the read
- evidence: research f2c7ccc2 §4 (Claude Code changelog quote; structural — no live PoC, the race window is narrow by nature); full suite green + conductor validation digest validation:v1:<sha> recorded in the shipping PR

### Disclose present-but-zero usage blocks instead of counting them as truth
- id: `rm-408` | track: correctness | priority: 62.0 | status: done (flipped at the integration commit gate 2026-10-05, conflict case 0b7e84df — round-2 review c5a2362a APPROVED over fix 2bff960d; the 880a7b9e/d8d5f193 reservation discharges here)
- signals: correctness.silent_zero_usage:crates/agenttrace-core/src/parser.rs::L4236-4310 (usage_from_value inserts every RECOGNIZED key even at value 0, and its tail guard `if usage.is_empty() { None } else { Some(usage) }` fires only when NO recognized key matched — so a present {input_tokens:0, output_tokens:0} returns Some(zeros) that flows into totals as measured truth; body byte-identical at ceiling c032f33 :4296), contrast crates/agenttrace-core/src/lib.rs::L585-590 (ABSENT usage gets the text-estimate fallback named estimated_from_text in provenance.tokens — the disclosure contract exists for the absent case only), ecosystem.corroboration: Claude Code CHANGELOG 2.1.288 — "Fixed long conversations failing with 'Prompt is too long' instead of auto-compacting when the last reply reported ZERO token usage" (the client's own fix proves zero-usage replies are a real transcript population; every pre-fix transcript carries them)
- acceptance: ONE stated rule for present-and-recognized-but-all-zero usage, not per-format accidents — EITHER count it with a provenance flag naming the affected-event count (extending the rm-054 provenance taxonomy) OR route it through the existing text-estimate fallback WITH disclosure; a contrast fixture pair pins the absent case (→ estimated_from_text) against the present-zero case (→ the new contract); no silent $0/0 undercount — a corpus of present-zero sessions surfaces the affected share in doctor and JSON, never reports clean zeros silently
- evidence: boundaries — rm-251 (ceiling) owns re-emission/id-less dedup and 1h-cache pricing (the double-count layer); rm-053's evidence refresh already names AgentMeasure issue #24's zero-token fixture ask (fixture layer — this item owns the semantics/disclosure contract); 41263f584891's rm-406 boundary line assigns "run 555a174d's N1" this layer (unknown-kind drops stay rm-406's); RC-1 kimi unknown-alias (6648588 ledger) is the no-key-match instance (→ None → estimate) — the recognized-zero branch is unowned elsewhere; parser read in-tree at base by research a9037c3c §4, anchors re-verified this attempt at base AND ceiling (function body identical, line drift only); grep "zero usage|zero token|all-zero" on wall+ceiling = only the rm-053 fixture note; conductor validation digest validation:v1:<sha> recorded in the shipping PR
- EXECUTED (2026-10-04, implement 349ab712 re-attested as 562afd0a @ worktree ea5c41e uncommitted — the 349ab712 fold rejection was KTD13 attestation-only, no code defect; validated targeted 1b450631 + full f093b7e5; NO test re-run at compound; review 342a1349 NEEDS_CHANGES repaired at fix 2bff960d 2026-10-05): crates/agenttrace-core/src/lib.rs adds usage_is_all_zero + Session.zero_usage_events (skip_serializing_if usize-is-zero) so present-but-zero usage blocks now COUNT with a `zero_usage_reported:<N>` suffix on provenance.tokens (arm (a) count-with-provenance extending the rm-054 taxonomy, NOT estimate-fallback); doctor.rs adds a zero_usage report block (session/event counts + up to six sanitized samples, JSON and text) plus an affected-session-share recommendation that also fires in demo mode (fix F8: the push was below the demo early-return); session_cache.rs round-trips the new counter with SESSION_CACHE_SCHEMA_VERSION bumped 22 → 23 (fix F1, rm-230 convention: a warm pre-rm-408 v22 entry parsed as fresh with the serde-defaulted counter at 0 — clean zeros; pinned red-first by stale_schema_22_cache_cannot_mask_the_disclosure, proven RED on a const-22 tree); census scopes to discovered session transcripts, sqlite-backed databases excluded (fix F9); acceptance legs: tests/zero_usage_contract.rs 4/0, core lib 158/0, discovery_contract 78/0 (schema pins at 23), tui 46/0 (warm-cache fixture at 23), live --demo --doctor -f json -> zero_usage {sessions: 200, events: 200} + share recommendation; full ci.yml mirror 20/20 rc0 (pre-review).

### Idle-aware hanging-anomaly classification — wall-clock idle must not read as agent latency
- id: `rm-422` | track: correctness | priority: 60.0 | status: candidate
- signals: correctness.idle_as_latency:crates/agenttrace-core/src/lib.rs::L841-852 (gaps computed over SORTED timestamps = wall-clock idle between events) + :888-912 (any gap >300s → "hanging" severity HIGH with detail "N gap(s) >60s"; the p95-latency anomaly at :906 counts the same idle-inclusive gaps) — a session idle over a weekend reports a HIGH-severity hanging anomaly; out-of-order journals cannot create negative gaps (sorted + >0 filter) but DO inflate spans; unclaimed on the wall (grep hanging/out-of-order = 0) and known-live since the 7e17ac1-era assessment F3 axis, re-verified at c032f33 this cycle
- acceptance: gap-based anomalies separate idle (user-absent wall-clock) from latency (in-flight spans such as tool-call→tool-result pairs): "hanging" fires only on in-flight spans, or its detail names the idle window explicitly; a weekend-idle fixture yields no HIGH anomaly (or a distinct idle class); out-of-order timestamps surface as their own diagnostic, never folded into latency; golden fixtures pin both arms
- evidence: assess 428195d6 A6 (anchors re-read at c032f33); conductor validation digest to be recorded at the implement gate

### Markdown report arm must not emit executable HTML from transcript-derived fields
- id: `rm-403` | track: security | priority: 74.0 | status: done
- signals: security.html_through_markdown:crates/agenttrace-core/src/reports.rs::L2569-2571 (markdown_cell escapes only `|` and newline — printable HTML passes through byte-for-byte into -f markdown output, and markdown renderers that allow inline HTML execute it), live PoC 2026-10-04 (assess 3e8493fa @7e17ac1): `--overview -f markdown -d <md-injection corpus>` → out.md:17,29 carry raw `<img onerror=…>` / `<script>…` sourced from transcript-derived model/session fields; the html arm already entity-escapes its metacharacters — this item owns the MARKDOWN arm only
- acceptance: -f markdown output contains no raw HTML tags from transcript-derived fields (entity-escape or neutralize per a documented policy), a hostile-corpus overview/sessions/diagnostics markdown re-renders inert (grep for `<img` / `<script` / `onerror` comes back escaped), and a regression fixture pins an `<img onerror>` payload in a model string; boundaries: control-byte sanitization is fc197c5e's co-pending sanitizer-family item (merge by title — its landed-uncommitted implement routes markdown_cell through sanitize_line_segment but leaves printable HTML intact by design), TSV/CSV formula injection is the 278b2bda/6e96bed5 lane, terminal text lanes are the landed rm-383 family; the governance `--audit -f markdown` arm (main.rs) embeds raw transcript-derived JSON inside a ```json fence — inert under CommonMark fence semantics (a mid-line triple backtick cannot close a fence; JSON strings carry no raw newlines; verified live by review 0145eeb5), so it is named as a boundary rather than fixed — a fence-unaware renderer could still render the JSON body (review finding 4)
- evidence: external precedent CC 2.1.289 changelog — 'published artifact pages freezing or crashing the reader's browser tab on short code blocks with many unclosed <script> tags' — the same threat class on a publish surface (research 802e0a09 snapshot cc-changelog.md); PoC artifacts preserved under /tmp/at-assess-3e8493fa/poc/; re-run the PoC against fc197c5e's binary at integration to keep the boundary honest; conductor validation digest validation:v1:<sha> recorded in the shipping PR
- cycle-1 implemented (2026-10-04, run 41263f584891 implement attempt 526be176 @ base 7e17ac1, uncommitted per phase boundary): markdown_cell now entity-escapes `& < >` BEFORE the pipe/newline table escapes, with a documented policy header + a merge note that the control-byte sanitizer family must run first at merge (control bytes, then printable HTML); the five raw Scope-row string fields (range / session-window timestamps / parse coverage / confidence) now route through markdown_cell too (pricing_source was already wrapped; the numeric coverage counts stay raw); markdown_inline_code keeps code-span semantics with the rationale documented (renderers escape code spans themselves; pre-escaping would corrupt display). Regression suite crates/agenttrace-core/tests/markdown_escape_contract.rs (2 tests: hostile payload arrives entity-escaped verbatim, `&`/`|`/newline display-faithful). Live PoC re-run on the assess corpus: raw `<img`/`<script` count 0 (was 2); lines 17/29 now carry `&lt;img src=x onerror=alert(1)&gt;` / `&lt;script&gt;alert(2)&lt;/script&gt;`. Status flip deferred to the commit gate (rm-012 precedent)

### OpenCode v2 fork accounting — session_v2/session_message semantics (ccusage #1780/#1782)
- id: `rm-507` | track: correctness | priority: 74.0 | status: candidate (added 2026-10-05, run 96762b67 research 1103a6a4)
- signals: correctness.v2_gap:crates/agenttrace-core/src/sqlite_sessions.rs::L358-386 (load_opencode_sqlite_sessions probes only the `session` table's authoritative aggregate columns — directory/cost/tokens_*; zero handling of OpenCode v2's session_v2/session_message schema, repo-wide grep for session_v2|fork_session_id|session_message = 0 hits), ecosystem.ccusage #1780/#1782 (v20.0.26, 2026-09-27): v2 stores sessions in a database; forks copy the parent's session_message rows with NEW ids but the parent's seq, marked by session_v2.fork_session_id + fork_boundary (through|before) pointing at the last copied message; a fork's session row is CUMULATIVE, so id-based dedupe double-counts the parent's full history under every fork in every aggregate; their shipped cutoff semantics: through→seq, before→seq-1, never drop when the boundary is unresolvable or the schema predates seq; an all-copied-rows fork must block the cumulative-aggregate fallback
- acceptance: the v2 schema is ingested (or explicitly disclosed as unsupported in --doctor with a corpus count either way); fork rows excluded from aggregates when the boundary resolves; unresolvable boundaries and seq-missing schemas disclose rather than silently sum; golden fixture db exercising parent+fork+through/before+nested forks; the existing `session`-table aggregate read's behavior on v2 databases characterized (cumulative fork rows corrected or disclosed); doctor reports the v2 corpus count; sequencing rider: lands on the same module as the rm-044 rusqlite jump — sequence or co-land
- evidence: ccusage #1780 report + #1782 fix semantics (api.github.com, fetched 2026-10-05); sqlite_sessions.rs + discovery.rs:799/834/858 read at HEAD 33cc9bb; conductor validation digest validation:v1:<sha> recorded in the shipping PR

### Bound the upstream-status subprocess waits
- id: `rm-543` | track: reliability | priority: 70.0 | status: implemented (2026-10-06 integration of run b1ff12f8, conflict case 8d388605)
- signals: reliability.unbounded_wait:crates/agenttrace-cli/src/upstream.rs::L184-199 (fetch_remote runs `git fetch` and git() runs git via Command::output() with no deadline — `agenttrace upstream status --fetch` on a stalled network hangs indefinitely), contrast: governance.rs GIT_PROBE_TIMEOUT 10s (rm-242, whose comment names "the same cap class as the network pricing fetch"), pricing.rs PRICING_DOWNLOAD cap (rm-167), explorer.rs CLIPBOARD_WAIT_BOUND 3s (rm-208)
- acceptance: both subprocess paths run under a stated deadline; expiry produces a terminal error naming the deadline and the git operation that timed out (never a silent partial report); the timeout constant is pinned by a unit test and documented in docs/guides/upstream-status.md; scripts/upstream-delta --fetch inherits the same bound or documents its dev-tool exemption
